-- Name: fn_apply_wallet_topup(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_apply_wallet_topup() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_wallet_id BIGINT;
    v_balance_before NUMERIC(14,2);
    v_balance_after NUMERIC(14,2);
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status <> 'paid' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.status = 'paid' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT id, balance
    INTO v_wallet_id, v_balance_before
    FROM wallets
    WHERE user_id = NEW.user_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF v_wallet_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Wallet not found for user %', NEW.user_id;
    END IF;

    UPDATE wallets
    SET balance = balance + NEW.amount,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = v_wallet_id;

    v_balance_after := v_balance_before + NEW.amount;

    INSERT INTO wallet_transactions(
        wallet_id, tx_type, direction, amount, balance_before, balance_after,
        status, ref_type, ref_id, description, created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        v_wallet_id, 'topup', 'credit', NEW.amount, v_balance_before, v_balance_after,
        'success', 'wallet_topup', NEW.id, 'Nạp tiền vào ví', NOW()
    )
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

    IF NEW.paid_at IS NULL THEN
        UPDATE wallet_topups
        SET paid_at = NOW(),
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE id = NEW.id;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_apply_wallet_topup() OWNER TO root;

-- Name: fn_auto_create_quiz_attempt(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_auto_create_quiz_attempt() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_attempt_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.attempt_id IS NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO quiz_attempts(user_id, quiz_id, score, started_at, created_at)
        VALUES (NEW.user_id, NEW.quiz_id, 0, NOW(), NOW())
        RETURNING id INTO v_attempt_id;

        NEW.attempt_id := v_attempt_id;
    ELSE
        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1
            FROM quiz_attempts qa
            WHERE qa.id = NEW.attempt_id
              AND qa.user_id = NEW.user_id
              AND qa.quiz_id = NEW.quiz_id
        ) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid attempt_id % for user % and quiz %', NEW.attempt_id, NEW.user_id, NEW.quiz_id;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_auto_create_quiz_attempt() OWNER TO root;

-- TOC entry 305 (class 1255 OID 17404)
-- Name: fn_handle_payment_paid(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_handle_payment_paid() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_user_id BIGINT;
    v_coupon_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status <> 'paid' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.status = 'paid' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;

    SELECT o.user_id, o.coupon_id INTO v_user_id, v_coupon_id FROM orders o WHERE o.id = NEW.order_id FOR UPDATE;
    IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Order % not found for payment %', NEW.order_id, NEW.id; END IF;

    UPDATE orders SET status = 'paid', paid_at = COALESCE(NEW.paid_at, NOW()), updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = NEW.order_id;
    IF NEW.paid_at IS NULL THEN UPDATE payments SET paid_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = NEW.id; END IF;

    IF v_coupon_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE coupons SET used_count = used_count + 1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = v_coupon_id AND is_active = TRUE AND expiry_date > NOW() AND (max_uses IS NULL OR used_count < max_uses);
        IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Coupon % is invalid, expired or max uses reached', v_coupon_id; END IF;
    END IF;

    -- [ĐÃ SỬA]: Đổi DO NOTHING thành DO UPDATE SET status = 'active' để mở khóa lại cho sinh viên mua lại
    INSERT INTO enrollments (user_id, course_id, enrolled_at, source, status)
    SELECT v_user_id, oi.course_id, NOW(), 'payment', 'active'
    FROM order_items oi WHERE oi.order_id = NEW.order_id
    ON CONFLICT (user_id, course_id) 
    DO UPDATE SET status = 'active', enrolled_at = NOW();

    INSERT INTO instructor_earnings (instructor_id, payment_id, course_id, amount, system_amount, created_at)
    SELECT c.instructor_id, NEW.id, oi.course_id, ROUND(oi.price * 0.70, 2), ROUND(oi.price * 0.30, 2), NOW()
    FROM order_items oi JOIN courses c ON c.id = oi.course_id WHERE oi.order_id = NEW.order_id
    ON CONFLICT (payment_id, course_id) DO NOTHING;

    INSERT INTO activity_logs(user_id, action, ref_type, ref_id, metadata, created_at)
    VALUES (v_user_id, 'buy_course', 'order', NEW.order_id, jsonb_build_object('payment_id', NEW.id, 'amount', NEW.amount), NOW());

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_handle_payment_paid() OWNER TO root;

-- TOC entry 399 (class 1255 OID 16979)
-- Name: fn_normalize_wallet_payment(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_normalize_wallet_payment() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.method <> 'wallet' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Only wallet payment method is allowed';
    END IF;

    NEW.provider := COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(NEW.provider), ''), 'wallet_core');

    IF NEW.provider_txn_id IS NULL OR LENGTH(TRIM(NEW.provider_txn_id)) = 0 THEN
        NEW.provider_txn_id := CONCAT('wallet_', gen_random_uuid()::TEXT);
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_normalize_wallet_payment() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 403 (class 1255 OID 17393)
-- Name: fn_prevent_duplicate_purchase(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_prevent_duplicate_purchase() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_user_id BIGINT;
    v_course_price NUMERIC(12,2);
BEGIN
    SELECT o.user_id INTO v_user_id FROM orders o WHERE o.id = NEW.order_id;
    IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Order % does not exist.', NEW.order_id; END IF;

    -- [ĐÃ SỬA]: Ép nó chỉ chặn nếu status đang là 'active'
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM enrollments e 
        WHERE e.user_id = v_user_id AND e.course_id = NEW.course_id AND e.status = 'active'
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User % already enrolled in course %', v_user_id, NEW.course_id;
    END IF;

    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM order_items oi JOIN orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
        WHERE o.user_id = v_user_id AND oi.course_id = NEW.course_id AND o.status IN ('pending', 'paid')
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User % already has course % in another pending/paid order', v_user_id, NEW.course_id;
    END IF;

    IF NEW.price IS NULL OR NEW.price = 0 THEN
        SELECT c.price INTO v_course_price FROM courses c WHERE c.id = NEW.course_id AND c.deleted_at IS NULL;
        IF v_course_price IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Course % not found or deleted', NEW.course_id; END IF;
        NEW.price := v_course_price;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_prevent_duplicate_purchase() OWNER TO root;

-- TOC entry 317 (class 1255 OID 17399)
-- Name: fn_process_admin_refund(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_process_admin_refund() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_payment RECORD; v_order RECORD; v_wallet_id BIGINT; v_balance_before NUMERIC(14,2);
    v_balance_after NUMERIC(14,2); v_refunded_before NUMERIC(12,2); v_refunded_after NUMERIC(12,2);
    v_remaining_amount NUMERIC(12,2); v_payment_status TEXT; v_coupon_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status <> 'approved' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.status = 'approved' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;

    SELECT p.* INTO v_payment FROM payments p WHERE p.id = NEW.payment_id FOR UPDATE;
    SELECT o.* INTO v_order FROM orders o WHERE o.id = v_payment.order_id FOR UPDATE;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pr.amount), 0) INTO v_refunded_before FROM payment_refunds pr WHERE pr.payment_id = NEW.payment_id AND pr.status = 'processed';
    
    v_refunded_after := v_refunded_before + NEW.amount;
    v_remaining_amount := v_payment.amount - v_refunded_after;
    IF v_remaining_amount = 0 THEN v_payment_status := 'refunded'; ELSE v_payment_status := 'partial_refunded'; END IF;

    SELECT w.id, w.balance INTO v_wallet_id, v_balance_before FROM wallets w WHERE w.user_id = v_order.user_id AND w.is_active = TRUE FOR UPDATE;
    UPDATE wallets SET balance = balance + NEW.amount, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = v_wallet_id;
    v_balance_after := v_balance_before + NEW.amount;

    UPDATE payments SET status = v_payment_status, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = v_payment.id;
    UPDATE orders SET status = CASE WHEN v_payment_status = 'refunded' THEN 'refunded' ELSE 'partial_refunded' END, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = v_order.id;

    v_coupon_id := v_order.coupon_id;

    -- [CHỖ SỬA ĐÂY]: Không dùng DELETE nữa, chỉ UPDATE trạng thái thu hồi
    IF NEW.refund_mode = 'revoke_access' THEN
        UPDATE enrollments e
        SET status = 'revoked'
        FROM order_items oi
        WHERE oi.order_id = v_order.id
          AND e.user_id = v_order.user_id
          AND e.course_id = oi.course_id;
    END IF;

    DELETE FROM instructor_earnings WHERE payment_id = v_payment.id;
    
    IF v_remaining_amount > 0 THEN
        INSERT INTO instructor_earnings (instructor_id, payment_id, course_id, amount, system_amount, created_at)
        SELECT c.instructor_id, v_payment.id, oi.course_id, ROUND((v_remaining_amount * oi.price / NULLIF(v_payment.amount, 0)) * 0.70, 2), ROUND((v_remaining_amount * oi.price / NULLIF(v_payment.amount, 0)) * 0.30, 2), NOW()
        FROM order_items oi JOIN courses c ON c.id = oi.course_id WHERE oi.order_id = v_order.id;
    END IF;

    UPDATE payment_refunds SET status = 'processed', processed_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = NEW.id;

    IF v_payment_status = 'refunded' AND v_coupon_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE coupons SET used_count = GREATEST(used_count - 1, 0), updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = v_coupon_id;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_process_admin_refund() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 368 (class 1255 OID 16981)
-- Name: fn_process_wallet_payment(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_process_wallet_payment() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_user_id BIGINT;
    v_order_total NUMERIC(12,2);
    v_wallet_id BIGINT;
    v_wallet_currency CHAR(3);
    v_wallet_balance NUMERIC(14,2);
    v_order_currency CHAR(3);
BEGIN
    IF NEW.method <> 'wallet' OR NEW.status <> 'paid' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.status = 'paid' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT o.user_id, o.total_amount, o.currency
    INTO v_user_id, v_order_total, v_order_currency
    FROM orders o
    WHERE o.id = NEW.order_id;

    IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Order % not found for wallet payment', NEW.order_id;
    END IF;

    IF NEW.amount <> v_order_total THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Wallet payment amount % must equal order total %', NEW.amount, v_order_total;
    END IF;

    SELECT w.id, w.currency, w.balance
    INTO v_wallet_id, v_wallet_currency, v_wallet_balance
    FROM wallets w
    WHERE w.user_id = v_user_id
      AND w.is_active = TRUE
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF v_wallet_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Active wallet not found for user %', v_user_id;
    END IF;

    IF v_wallet_currency <> v_order_currency THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Currency mismatch: wallet % vs order %', v_wallet_currency, v_order_currency;
    END IF;

    IF v_wallet_balance < NEW.amount THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Insufficient wallet balance: need %, have %', NEW.amount, v_wallet_balance;
    END IF;

    UPDATE wallets
    SET balance = balance - NEW.amount,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = v_wallet_id;

    INSERT INTO wallet_transactions(
        wallet_id, tx_type, direction, amount, balance_before, balance_after,
        status, ref_type, ref_id, description, created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        v_wallet_id,
        'purchase',
        'debit',
        NEW.amount,
        v_wallet_balance,
        v_wallet_balance - NEW.amount,
        'success',
        'payment',
        NEW.id,
        'Thanh toán đơn hàng bằng ví',
        NOW()
    )
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_process_wallet_payment() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 390 (class 1255 OID 17395)
-- Name: fn_recalculate_order_totals(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_recalculate_order_totals() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_order_id BIGINT;
    v_subtotal NUMERIC(12,2);
    v_discount_percent NUMERIC(5,2);
    v_discount NUMERIC(12,2);
BEGIN
    v_order_id := COALESCE(NEW.order_id, OLD.order_id);

    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(price), 0)
    INTO v_subtotal
    FROM order_items
    WHERE order_id = v_order_id;

    SELECT c.discount_percent
    INTO v_discount_percent
    FROM orders o
    LEFT JOIN coupons c ON c.id = o.coupon_id
    WHERE o.id = v_order_id;

    v_discount := ROUND(v_subtotal * COALESCE(v_discount_percent, 0) / 100.0, 2);

    UPDATE orders
    SET subtotal_amount = v_subtotal,
        discount_amount = v_discount,
        total_amount = v_subtotal - v_discount,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = v_order_id;

    RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_recalculate_order_totals() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 330 (class 1255 OID 17410)
-- Name: fn_refresh_ratings(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_refresh_ratings() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_course_id BIGINT;
    v_instructor_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
    v_course_id := COALESCE(NEW.course_id, OLD.course_id);

    UPDATE courses c
    SET rating_avg = COALESCE((
        SELECT ROUND(AVG(r.rating)::NUMERIC, 2)
        FROM reviews r
        WHERE r.course_id = v_course_id
    ), 0),
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE c.id = v_course_id;

    SELECT c.instructor_id INTO v_instructor_id
    FROM courses c
    WHERE c.id = v_course_id;

    IF v_instructor_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE instructor_profiles ip
        SET rating_avg = COALESCE((
            SELECT ROUND(AVG(r.rating)::NUMERIC, 2)
            FROM reviews r
            JOIN courses c ON c.id = r.course_id
            WHERE c.instructor_id = v_instructor_id
        ), 0),
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE ip.user_id = v_instructor_id;
    END IF;

    RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_refresh_ratings() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 324 (class 1255 OID 17408)
-- Name: fn_update_course_progress(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_update_course_progress() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_course_id BIGINT;
    v_total_lessons INT;
    v_completed_lessons INT;
    v_percent NUMERIC(5,2);
BEGIN
    SELECT cs.course_id INTO v_course_id
    FROM lessons l
    JOIN course_sections cs ON cs.id = l.section_id
    WHERE l.id = NEW.lesson_id;

    IF v_course_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_total_lessons
    FROM lessons l
    JOIN course_sections cs ON cs.id = l.section_id
    WHERE cs.course_id = v_course_id
      AND l.deleted_at IS NULL;

    IF v_total_lessons = 0 THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_completed_lessons
    FROM lesson_progress lp
    JOIN lessons l ON l.id = lp.lesson_id
    JOIN course_sections cs ON cs.id = l.section_id
    WHERE lp.user_id = NEW.user_id
      AND lp.is_completed = TRUE
      AND cs.course_id = v_course_id
      AND l.deleted_at IS NULL;

    v_percent := ROUND((v_completed_lessons::NUMERIC * 100.0) / v_total_lessons, 2);

    INSERT INTO course_progress(user_id, course_id, progress_percent, created_at, updated_at)
    VALUES (NEW.user_id, v_course_id, v_percent, NOW(), NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (user_id, course_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        progress_percent = EXCLUDED.progress_percent,
        updated_at = NOW();

    IF v_percent >= 100 THEN
        INSERT INTO activity_logs(user_id, action, ref_type, ref_id, metadata, created_at)
        VALUES (
            NEW.user_id,
            'complete_course',
            'course',
            v_course_id,
            jsonb_build_object('progress_percent', v_percent),
            NOW()
        );
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_update_course_progress() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 328 (class 1255 OID 17391)
-- Name: fn_validate_course_publish(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_validate_course_publish() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_lessons_count INT;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'published' AND COALESCE(OLD.status, 'draft') <> 'published' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_lessons_count
        FROM lessons l
        JOIN course_sections cs ON cs.id = l.section_id
        WHERE cs.course_id = NEW.id
          AND l.deleted_at IS NULL;

        IF v_lessons_count = 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot publish course % without lessons', NEW.id;
        END IF;

        IF NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN
            NEW.published_at := NOW();
        END IF;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_validate_course_publish() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 363 (class 1255 OID 17406)
-- Name: fn_validate_lesson_progress(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_validate_lesson_progress() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    v_course_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
    SELECT cs.course_id INTO v_course_id
    FROM lessons l
    JOIN course_sections cs ON cs.id = l.section_id
    WHERE l.id = NEW.lesson_id;

    IF v_course_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Lesson % not found', NEW.lesson_id;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM enrollments e
        WHERE e.user_id = NEW.user_id
          AND e.course_id = v_course_id
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User % is not enrolled in course %', NEW.user_id, v_course_id;
    END IF;

    IF NEW.is_completed = TRUE AND (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR OLD.is_completed = FALSE) THEN
        NEW.completed_at := COALESCE(NEW.completed_at, NOW());
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_validate_lesson_progress() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 412 (class 1255 OID 17397)
-- Name: fn_validate_payment_transition(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_validate_payment_transition() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
        IF OLD.status = 'failed' AND NEW.status IN ('paid', 'partial_refunded', 'refunded') THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Payment state transition from failed is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'refunded' AND NEW.status <> 'refunded' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Payment state transition from refunded is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'partial_refunded' AND NEW.status = 'pending' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Payment state transition partial_refunded -> pending is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'partial_refunded' AND NEW.status = 'paid' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Payment state transition partial_refunded -> paid is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'paid' AND NEW.status = 'pending' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Payment state transition paid -> pending is not allowed';
        END IF;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_validate_payment_transition() OWNER TO root;

--
-- TOC entry 322 (class 1255 OID 17398)
-- Name: fn_validate_refund_transition(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: root
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.fn_validate_refund_transition() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
        IF OLD.status = 'processed' AND NEW.status <> 'processed' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refund state transition from processed is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'rejected' AND NEW.status <> 'rejected' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refund state transition from rejected is not allowed';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'pending' AND NEW.status NOT IN ('approved', 'rejected', 'pending') THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refund transition pending -> % is not allowed', NEW.status;
        END IF;

        IF OLD.status = 'approved' AND NEW.status NOT IN ('approved', 'processed') THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refund transition approved -> % is not allowed', NEW.status;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.fn_validate_refund_transition() OWNER TO root;

    coupon_id bigint,

    currency character(3) DEFAULT 'VND'::bpchar NOT NULL,
    status text DEFAULT 'pending'::text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    paid_at timestamp with time zone,
    CONSTRAINT chk_orders_status CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::text, 'paid'::text, 'partial_refunded'::text, 'refunded'::text, 'cancelled'::text, 'expired'::text]))),
    CONSTRAINT orders_check CHECK ((subtotal_amount >= discount_amount)),
    CONSTRAINT orders_check1 CHECK ((total_amount = (subtotal_amount - discount_amount))),
    CONSTRAINT orders_discount_amount_check CHECK ((discount_amount >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT orders_subtotal_amount_check CHECK ((subtotal_amount >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT orders_total_amount_check CHECK ((total_amount >= (0)::numeric))
);

    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    amount numeric(14,2) NOT NULL,
    method text NOT NULL,
    provider text,
    provider_txn_id text,

    CONSTRAINT wallet_topups_amount_check CHECK ((amount > (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_topups_method_check CHECK ((method = ANY (ARRAY['card'::text, 'momo'::text, 'bank_transfer'::text, 'crypto'::text]))),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_topups_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::text, 'paid'::text, 'failed'::text, 'cancelled'::text])))
);

    tx_type text NOT NULL,
    direction text NOT NULL,

    balance_before numeric(14,2) NOT NULL,
    balance_after numeric(14,2) NOT NULL,
    status text DEFAULT 'success'::text NOT NULL,
    ref_type text,
    ref_id bigint,
    description text,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_amount_check CHECK ((amount > (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_balance_after_check CHECK ((balance_after >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_balance_before_check CHECK ((balance_before >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_direction_check CHECK ((direction = ANY (ARRAY['credit'::text, 'debit'::text]))),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::text, 'success'::text, 'failed'::text]))),
    CONSTRAINT wallet_transactions_tx_type_check CHECK ((tx_type = ANY (ARRAY['topup'::text, 'purchase'::text, 'refund'::text, 'adjustment'::text])))
);
